:::info

- Ultimate, Professional, Home Premium, Home Basic, Starter, and their N and E version ISOs (except August 2018) contain all editions except Enterprise. However, by default, the other editions do not appear during Windows installation. You can easily choose any edition during installation by deleting or editing the `sources\ei.cfg` file in the bootable USB or ISO.

:::
